- **strategy-evaluation**: **Five Pillars of Strategy Evaluation:** ``` 1. Statistical Significance — Is the edge real or noise? 2. - **strategy_ladder_buys**: This skill should be used when invoked by master_trading with the ladder-buys strategy enabled, or when the user asks to "apply ladder buys", "check ladder rungs", "buy on the dip". ([khoks/ClaudeTrading](https://github.com/khoks/ClaudeTrading/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/strategy_ladder_buys) · ★4)
- **strategy_ladder_buys_v2**: Internal sub-skill of master_trading_v2; called mechanically by tick.sh during Phase B. Same logic as v1 strategy_ladder_buys (broad: any buyable that drops past drop_percent from last_buy gets a notional buy, with sizing capped by max_per_trade_usd). Operators don't normally invoke this directly — use /master_trading_v2. ([khoks/ClaudeTrading](https://github.com/khoks/ClaudeTrading/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/strategy_ladder_buys_v2) · ★4)
- **strategy_mean_reversion**: This skill should be used when invoked by master_trading with the mean_reversion strategy enabled, or when the user asks to "buy the laggard", "find the worst performer", "apply mean reversion". Cross-sectional, contrarian. Compares each buyable stock's N-day return against the pool's median return; the worst k laggards (those falling at least min_underperformance_percent below the median) get a small buy. Falling-knife guard via 50-day moving average; rebuy throttle via min_hours_between_buys on top of the 2-trading-day H1B floor. ([khoks/ClaudeTrading](https://github.com/khoks/ClaudeTrading/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/strategy_mean_reversion) · ★4)

Operators don't normally invoke this directly — use /master_trading_v2. ([khoks/ClaudeTrading](https://github.com/khoks/ClaudeTrading/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/strategy_mean_reversion_v2) · ★4)
- **strategy_profit_take**: This skill should be used when invoked by master_trading with the profit_take strategy enabled, or when the user asks to "scale out", "take profits", "lock in gains", "tier-out my position". Eager partial-exit on absolute gain. For each stock in the sellable set, sells a fraction of the original (baseline) position when current gain hits each threshold in a configured ladder. Pairs with trailing_stop — profit_take captures gains as they happen on the way up; trailing_stop captures the residual position after a real reversal. ([khoks/ClaudeTrading](https://github.com/khoks/ClaudeTrading/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/strategy_profit_take) · ★4)

([kamesh231/billingplane](https://github.com/kamesh231/billingplane/tree/HEAD/skills/stripe-billing))
- **stripe-billing**: Use this skill any time you touch checkout, subscriptions, the Stripe webhook handler, pricing/plan config, or any payment-related code. Stripe subscription billing ships built into this template by default — this skill covers how to keep it correct and safe, not whether to add it. ([on-codes/builder-lab-template](https://github.com/on-codes/builder-lab-template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/stripe-billing))
- **stripe-billing-context**: Creates or updates a stripe-billing-context.md with business model, Stripe account, products/prices, webhook endpoints, email flows, and KPI targets. Use FIRST for a new Stripe account or when other skills need business context. Triggers: "set up stripe context", "billing brief", "document our Stripe setup".
